# Jacob's Ticket
Aliases: Jacob Ticket
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Farming
[Jacob's Ticket]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Events Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 10,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JACOBS_TICKET
Tooltip: UNCOMMON
Jacob's Ticket is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that act as awards for participation in [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]] and can be used to purchase items at [[Anita]]'s shop and for some crafting recipes including farming tools.
## Obtaining
### [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]]
|Placement|# of Tickets|
|-|-|
|DIAMOND|35|
|PLATINUM|30|
|GOLD|25|
|SILVER|15|
|BRONZE|10|
|Participated|1|
### Anita
[[Medals]] can also be converted for Jacob's Tickets in [[Anita]]'s menu, this is however not recommended unless the player is [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]].
|Medal|Tickets|
|-|-|
|Gold|[[Jacob's Ticket]] 16 Jacob's Tickets|
|Silver|[[Jacob's Ticket]] 4 Jacob's Tickets|
|Bronze|[[Jacob's Ticket]] 2 Jacob's Tickets|
### Super Jacob Entertainment System
When placed in a [[Farming]] [[Minions|Minion]], the [[Super Jacob Entertainment System]] will generate [[Jacob's Ticket]] 1 Jacob's Ticket every 3h. This item can only be placed in one minion per profile.
## Usage
### Merchants
[[Anita]] sells various farming tools and permanent profile upgrades which can be purchased with Tickets or a combination of Jacob's [[Medals]] and Tickets.
### Abiphones
[[Jacob]] requires being given [[Jacob's Ticket]] 10 Jacob's Tickets for his [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.

# Jake's Plushie
Aliases: Jake’s Plushie
Categories: Uncommon items, Accessories
[Jake's Plushie]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JAKE_PLUSHIE
Tooltip: Health: +2; Accessory Power: +5; A mystery was solved!; Increases the number of pelts obtained when hunting animals by +1.; * Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
Jake's Plushie is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]].
## Obtaining
Jake's Plushie is obtained by completing visitor requests for [[Beth]] in [[The Garden]].
After completing three requests, [[Beth]] will ask the player to visit [[Coordinates|175, 42, -470]] in the [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]]. After breaking the block at the coordinates, the player will find a secret lab, where they're asked to report back to [[Beth]] the next time she visits the player's garden. They return to the location to confront [[Jake]], who then rewards them with the accessory.
Once obtained, it can be purchased from [[Rusty]] for 15,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Jake's Plushie [[Health]]: +2
[Effects]
[Increases the number of Pelts obtained when hunting animals by +1.]
## Trivia
- The accessory was discovered on January 31st, 2023, by player 2[[Ranks|[VIP]]] Zitronbtw.
- The 1 [[Pelts|Pelt]] stacks with the [[Pelt Belt]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jan 26, 2023|Added Jake's Plushie.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Feb 14, 2023|0.18|Added Jake's Plushie.|
|Mar 8, 2023|0.18.1|Jake's Plushie is now [[Soulbound]].|
|Mar 8, 2023|0.18.1|Jake's Plushie can now be bought from [[Rusty]].|

# Jelly
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients
[Jelly]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes

Sacks: [[Garden Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 100 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JELLY
Tooltip: Can be spread across a Garden plot with a Sprayonator or used in Pest Traps.; Attracts: Praying Mantis, Dragonfly, Firefly; UNCOMMON
Jelly is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can be used with a [[Sprayonator]] to attract [[Pests]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Field Mouse]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv1]|[[Beetle]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Cricket]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Dragonfly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Earthworm]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Firefly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Fly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Locust]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Mite]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Mosquito]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Moth]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Praying Mantis]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Rat]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Slug]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
### Farming
Jelly can also be obtained from the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Orchid Mantis Pet]]'s Orchid Nectar perk, up to a 0.02% (1 in 5000) chance when farming crops.
### Attributes
[[Attributes|Visitor Jelly]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants up to 2.5% chance for [[Garden Visitors|Visitors]] to bring Jelly, which can be further increased with [[Attributes|Queenly Echo]] attribute.
[[Attributes|Sprayonator Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants 10% chance for [[Pests]] to drop an extra [[Sprayonator|Sprayonator Material]].
## Usage
### Attracting Pests
Jelly can be used with a [[Sprayonator]] to attract [[Praying Mantis|Praying Mantises]], [[Dragonfly|Dragonflies]], and [[Firefly|Fireflies]] to a sprayed plot.
Jelly can also be used as bait in a [[Pest Trap]], [[Mouse Trap]], or [[Vermin Trap]] allowing them to catch [[Pests]] and increasing the chance to catch a [[Praying Mantis]], [[Dragonfly]], or [[Firefly]] by 12x.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Royal Jelly]]|None|64x [[Floral Gelatin]] [[Jelly]] 64x Jelly|Recipe: [[Floral Gelatin]] x8 ×8 + Jelly x64 → [[Royal Jelly]]|

Item ID: JERRY_BOX_GOLDEN
Tooltip: Obtained while Mayor Jerry is elected.; Contains coins, Skill XP and posh jerry items.; Right-click to open!; UNCOMMON
Jerry Boxes are loot boxes dropped from [[Hidden Jerry|Hidden Jerries]] while [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]] is active as mayor.
## Obtaining
While [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]] is in office, there is a chance that a [[Hidden Jerry]] will appear after the player chops logs, breaks crops, mines blocks or kills [[Mob|Mobs]]. Killing a [[Hidden Jerry]] drops a Jerry Box corresponding to its rarity. There is a 6 minute cooldown after finding a Hidden Jerry before another one will spawn.
|Slot|Color|Weight|Odds|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Jerry Box]]|Green|12|54.55% (1 in 1.83)|
|[[Jerry Box]]|Blue|6|27.27% (1 in 3.67)|
|[[Jerry Box]]|Purple|3|13.64% (1 in 7.33)|
|[[Jerry Box]]|Golden|1|4.55% (1 in 22)|
Golden Jerry spawn chance can be increased with [[Tracking]].
Golden Jerry Box can also be obtained as a MINOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]], or from the [[Gift Bag of the Century]].
## Drops
Right-clicking with a Jerry Box in hand opens a menu from which the player can open the box. Drops include [[Coins]], [[Skills|Skill]] XP and unique [[Jerry]] items.

|[[Jerry 3D Glasses]]|1|0.81%|
All Jerry Boxes drop the same Jerry items (with the exception that Golden Jerry Boxes cannot drop a [[Green Jerry Talisman]]), but higher rarity boxes will drop more coins and Skill XP.
## Trivia
- The Golden Jerry Box appears to have a crown.
- Despite [[Mayor Election|Mayor]] candidate [[Derpy]]'s +50% skill experience buff, the boxes still grant the same amount of [[Skills|Skill]] XP if they are opened during his mayorship.
- Farming [[Nether Wart|Nether Warts]] used to not spawn Hidden Jerries, presumably because it used to give [[Alchemy|Alchemy XP]] instead of [[Farming|Farming XP]].
- Jerry Boxes are an effective way to turn [[Coins]] into [[Skills|Skill XP]].
  - Green Jerry Boxes cost per [[Skills|Skill XP]], or for level LX.
  - Blue Jerry Boxes cost per [[Skills|Skill XP]], or for level LX.
  - Purple Jerry Boxes cost per [[Skills|Skill XP]], or for level LX.
  - Golden Jerry Boxes cost per [[Skills|Skill XP]], or for level LX.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 11, 2021|Added Jerry Box.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Fixed Jerry Boxes not dropping from Hidden Jerries ([[Hidden Jerry]]) when dying from fall damage, void damage, and fire damage.|
|May 6, 2026|0.24.5|Jerry Boxes to now go into the inventory instead of being left on the floor.|

# Jerry Candy
Aliases: Jerry Candy Potion
Categories: Uncommon items, Items
[Jerry Candy]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JERRY_CANDY
Tooltip: Right-click to consume and gain the Jerry Effect (20:00):; +100 Health +20 Strength +2 Ferocity +100 Intelligence +3 Magic Find; UNCOMMON
The Jerry Candy is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] consumable that grants the user several stat bonuses for 20 minutes.
## Obtaining
### Jerry Boxes
Jerry Candy is obtainable from opening [[Jerry Box|Jerry Boxes]].
|Jerry Box|Chance|
|-|-|
|Green ([[Jerry Box]])|6.61%|
|Blue ([[Jerry Box]])|6.61%|
|Purple ([[Jerry Box]])|6.47%|
### Jerry Pet
Jerry Candy also rarely drops from mobs with a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Jerry Pet]] equipped. The chance of obtaining it as a mob drop is around 1/500 (0.2%). It is not affected by [[Magic Find]].
## Usage
The Jerry Candy can be consumed to grant the Jerry effect, which grants the following stats for 20 minutes:
- +100 [[Health]]
- +20 [[Strength]]
- +2 [[Ferocity]]
- +100 [[Intelligence]]
- +3 [[Magic Find]]
## Trivia
- The Jerry Candy is not considered a potion, and thus isn't affected by [[Potion Affinity Talisman]] (or any upgrades) or [[Alchemy]] levels.
- The Jerry Candy effect is granted by [[God Potion|God Potions]] for its full duration, despite not being considered a potion. Additionally, Jerry Candies cannot be consumed while a [[God Potion]] is active.
- You cannot stack effects of multiple Jerry Candies.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 11, 2021|Added Jerry Candy.|

# Jerry Stone

Req. Skill Level: Mining I (1) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: JERRY_STONE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Jerry's reforge when combined with Aspect of the Jerry and Aspect of the Jerry, Signature Edition.; Use with your favorite Aspect of the Jerry.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Crit Chance  Crit Damage; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 1.; UNCOMMON REFORGE STONE
The Jerry Stone is a [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Jerry's]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to an [[Aspect of the Jerry]] (and [[Aspect of the Jerry, Signature Edition|Signature Edition]]). It grants [[Crit Chance]], [[Crit Damage]], and [[Strength]].
## Obtaining
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
The Jerry Stone can be dropped from any [[Jerry Box]], which can drop from [[Hidden Jerry|Hidden Jerries]], that spawn while Jerry is selected as Mayor
## Usage
### Reforging
Jerry Stone can be combined with an [[Aspect of the Jerry]] or [[Aspect of the Jerry, Signature Edition]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Jerry's]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]] and [[Crit Chance]], which don't scale with the Sword's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining I (1)]]. A Sword that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Jerry's]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
The Jerry Stone replaces the [[Aspect of the Jerry]]'s Parley ability with No Parley, which consumes all of the player's [[Mana]] and adds 10% of that amount as [[Damage]] on the next hit with the Sword. This is very similar to the [[End Stone Sword]] and [[End Stone Bow]]'s abilities, Extreme Focus.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+25 Str ([[Strength]]) +50 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +10 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|+25 Str ([[Strength]]) +50 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +10 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|+25 Str ([[Strength]]) +50 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +10 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|+25 Str ([[Strength]]) +50 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +10 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|+25 Str ([[Strength]]) +50 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +10 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|+25 Str ([[Strength]]) +50 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +10 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|+25 Str ([[Strength]]) +50 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +10 CC ([[Crit Chance]])|
|Cost to apply|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The Jerry Stone is the only reforge stone that changes the ability of the item it is applied to.
- Before 0.27.2, it also had the lowest apply cost, costing 1 [[Coins|Coin]] to apply to a common Aspect of the Jerry.
- The No Parley ability used to add 100% of the player's [[Mana]], allowing players to hit for billions of damage.

|Sep 30, 2026|Changed rarity from RARE ([[Rarity]]) to UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

A Junk Sinker is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Rod Parts|Rod Part]].
## Obtaining
A Junk Sinker is given to the player as part of the introduction to the [[Backwater Bayou]] by [[Junker Joel]].
A Junk Sinker can also be purchased from [[Junker Joel]] for 10,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
A Junk Sinker can be applied to any [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] at [[Roddy]]. While equipped and the player is in the [[Backwater Bayou]], it grants +10 [[Treasure Chance]] and replaces [[Treasure]] pool items with Junk.
### Junk
Junk can be fished up in the [[Backwater Bayou]] with [[Junk Sinker]] Junk Sinker applied to the [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]].

Item ID: SALMON_OPAL
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Headstrong reforge when combined with a Bow.; https:\ \ en.wikipedia.org\ wiki\ Opal.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Crit Chance  Crit Damage; Headstrong Bonus Deal +8%  Damage when hitting a mob's head.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 15.; UNCOMMON REFORGE STONE
https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opal
Kaleidoscopic Mineral is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Headstrong]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Bows]], which grants [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]] and [[Crit Chance]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv61]|[[Treasure Hoarder]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
## Usage
### Reforging
Kaleidoscopic Mineral can be applied to [[Bows]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Headstrong]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]] and [[Crit Chance]], which scale with the bow's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XV (15)]]. A bow that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Headstrong]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Headstrong Bonus, which grants +8% more damage when hitting a mob's head.

## Trivia
- The description of this [[Reforge Stones|reforge stone]] used to contain the first two paragraphs of the Opal Wikipedia article. This was removed due to lag issues and dupe glitches because of the massive size of the item description. The former complete lore can be viewed below.
Opal is a hydrated amorphous form of silica (SiO2·nH2O); its water content may range from 3 to 21% by weight, but is usually between 6 and 10%. Because of its amorphous character, it is classed as a mineraloid, unlike crystalline forms of silica, which are classed as minerals. It is deposited at a relatively low temperature and may occur in the fissures of almost any kind of rock, being most commonly found with limonite, sandstone, rhyolite, marl, and basalt. There are two broad classes of opal: precious and common. Precious opal displays play-of-color (iridescence), common opal does not. Play-of-color is defined as “a pseudo chromatic optical effect resulting in flashes of colored light from certain minerals, as they are turned in white light.“ The internal structure of precious opal causes it to diffract light, resulting in play-of-color. Depending on the conditions in which it formed, opal may be transparent, translucent, or opaque and the background color may be white, black, or nearly any color of the visual spectrum. Black opal is considered to be the rarest, whereas white, gray, and green are the most common.

## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Keeled Slug Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Slug]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Keeled Slug Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Keeled Slug Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Bonus Pest Chance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Bonus Pest Chance ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Bonus Pest Chance]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

## Obtaining
Kuudra Chunk can be obtained from the [[Kuudra]] collection.
|Name|Collection|
|-|-|
|Kuudra Chunk|Kuudra II (2) ([[Kuudra]])|
|Meaty Kuudra Chunk|Kuudra III (3) ([[Kuudra]])|
|Hardened Kuudra Chunk|Kuudra IV (4) ([[Kuudra]])|
|Enriched Kuudra Chunk|Kuudra V (5) ([[Kuudra]])|
## Usage
Kuudra Chunk can be used to upgrade the rarity of the [[Kuudra Pet]]. A Kuudra Chunk can be right-clicked on an equipped [[Kuudra Pet]] to upgrade it to the following rarity. The item will not be consumed upon upgrading a pet.
|Name|Buff|
|-|-|
|Kuudra Chunk|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|
|Meaty Kuudra Chunk|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|
|Hardened Kuudra Chunk|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|
|Enriched Kuudra Chunk|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|
## Trivia
- For a while after it was released, the Kuudra Chunk's lore would say "Right click on your Kuudra Pet to apply this skin!" despite the item not being a [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]].

## Obtaining
### Mob Drops

|[Lv6]|[[Lapis Zombie]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|

## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Lapis Armor Helmet ([[Lapis Armor]]); [[Defense]]: +25 [[Mining Speed]]: +20 [[Mining Fortune]]: +2 Piece Bonus: Magnetic; Earn +50% more Exp when mining.|Lapis Armor Chestplate ([[Lapis Armor]]); [[Defense]]: +40 [[Mining Speed]]: +20 [[Mining Fortune]]: +2 Piece Bonus: Magnetic; Earn +50% more Exp when mining.|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Lapis Armor Leggings ([[Lapis Armor]]); [[Defense]]: +35 [[Mining Speed]]: +20 [[Mining Fortune]]: +2 Piece Bonus: Magnetic; Earn +50% more Exp when mining.|Lapis Armor Boots ([[Lapis Armor]]); [[Defense]]: +20 [[Mining Speed]]: +20 [[Mining Fortune]]: +2 Piece Bonus: Magnetic; Earn +50% more Exp when mining.|
[Total]
[Lapis Armor Defense: +120 Mining Speed: +80 Mining Fortune: +8]
## Trivia
- It is a popular choice among early game players, as the +200% Bonus experience when mining ores is very good for grinding experience, and the +120 [[Defense]] makes it slightly better than diamond armor.
- Using [[Luck]] on a weapon, players have higher odds of obtaining the Lapis Armor Set.
- The Lapis Zombies' Helmet matches the Lapis Armor Helmet only when they are using their ranged attacks. When they are not, their helmet is a Blue Stained Glass block.

# Lapis Crystal
Aliases: Magnetic
Categories: Uncommon items, Reforge stones, Mob Drops
[Lapis Crystal]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]

Applied To: Mining Tools ([[Tools]])
Apply Cost: 25,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining V (5) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 50 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LAPIS_CRYSTAL
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Magnetic reforge when combined with a Mining Tool.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Mining Speed; Magnetic Bonus Earn +100% more Exp when mining.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 5.; UNCOMMON REFORGE STONE
The Lapis Crystal is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Magnetic]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] or the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants [[Mining Speed]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv6]|[[Lapis Zombie]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[Lv6]|[[Lapis Zombie (Stranded)]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
## Usage
### Reforging
Lapis Crystal can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Magnetic]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Speed]], which scales with the Mining Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining V (5)]]. A Mining Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Magnetic]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Magnetic Bonus, which grants 100% more [[Experience|Exp]] when mining.

|Cost to apply|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|

- Before 0.27.2, the Lapis Crystal's texture was a recolored [[Diamond Atom]]/[[Midas Jewel]].

The Large Walnut is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Earthy]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Farming Tools]]. It grants [[Farming Fortune]].
## Obtaining
The Large Walnut can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 150 [[Copper]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Large Walnut can be combined with a [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Earthy]] reforge, to grant [[Farming Fortune]], which scales with the Farming Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XII (12)]]. A Farming Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Earthy]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Earthy Bonus, which grants 5% more ✿ [[Sowdust]] from Farming.

The Lava Fishing Sack is a [[Sacks|Sack]] unlocked in the [[Magmafish]] collection at [[Magmafish|Magmafish VI (6)]], [[Magmafish|Magmafish IX (9)]], and [[Magmafish|Magmafish XI (11)]] with increasing capacity. The Light Blue Sheep Skin is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Light Blue Sheep Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on November 7, 2020. Being [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 250 purchases.
## Usage
The Light Blue Sheep Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Sheep Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Litterbug Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Litterbug]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Litterbug Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Litterbug Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Buckle Fisher]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Buckle Fisher ([[Attributes]]); Junk catches are +1%–10% more likely to be GREAT.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Lotus is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Fishing]] [[Collections|Collection]] item obtained in various ways on the [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|25|[[Froggles]] Recipe|+4|
|II|100|[[Red Sweater]] Recipe|+4|
|III|250|[[Silver Lotus]] Recipe|+4|

|VII|10,000|[[Golden Froggles]] Recipe|+4|

|IX|50,000|[[Trophy Line]] Recipe|+4|
|X|100,000|[[Diamond Lotus]] Recipe|+4|
|XI|250,000|[[Diamond Froggles]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
### Fishing
Lotus has a 15.38% chance to be fished up from any water source block on the [[Lotus Atoll]] in the normal catch [[Treasure]] pool.
### Lily Pads
Exploding [[Lotus Atoll|Lily Pads]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]] gives [[Lotus]] 60–100 (1 stack plus 36) Lotus.
### Mob Drops

|[Lv1]|[[Lotusfish]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
### Trophy Frogs
Lotus can also obtained by [[Researcher Ribery|Donating]] [[Trophy Frogs]] to [[Researcher Ribery]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]].

|[[Lava Water Orb]]|None|1x [[Water Orb]] 2x [[Thunder Fragment]] 16x [[Horn of Taurus]] 32x [[Silver Magmafish]] 32x [[Flaming Heart]] 32x [[Cup of Blood]] [[Lump of Magma]] 64x Lump of Magma 64x [[Moogma Pelt]]|Recipe: [[Silver Magmafish]] x16 ×2 + [[Thunder Fragment]] x2 + [[Moogma Pelt]] x64 + [[Water Orb]] + Lump of Magma x64 + [[Cup of Blood]] x32 + [[Horn of Taurus]] x16 + [[Flaming Heart]] x32 → [[Lava Water Orb]]|
### Essence Crafting
Lumps of Magma are used to add [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] to pieces of gear:
- [[Magma Rod]]
- [[Inferno Rod]]
- [[Hellfire Rod]]
- [[Blade of the Volcano]]
- [[Slug Boots]]
- [[Ichthyic Set]]
- [[Finwave Set]]
- [[Gillsplash Set]]

## Obtaining
Lushlilac is obtained from clicking Lushlilac bushes around [[Moonglade Marsh]]. The [[Heart of the Forest]] perk [[Heart of the Forest|Collector]] and [[Attributes|Yummy]] attribute both grant a 2–100% chance to drop double Lushlilacs.

Consuming a Lushlilac grants +0.5 [[Charm Chance]] for 8 hours.

|[[Brine Tonic]]|None|1x [[Sea Brine]] [[Lushlilac]] 32x Lushlilac 32x [[Wet Water]]|Recipe: Lushlilac x8 ×4 + [[Wet Water]] x8 ×4 + [[Sea Brine]] → [[Brine Tonic]]|

# Luxurious Spool
Aliases: Silky
Categories: Uncommon items, Power stones, Power Stones, Mob Drops
Older Spiders often make better quality string. It is said that female ones are even more sturdy, but it was never proven.
[Luxurious Spool]
Type: Accessory Power Stone
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Older Spiders often make better quality string. It is said that female ones are even more sturdy, but it was never proven.
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Power: Silky
Req. Skill Level: Combat XV (15) ([[Combat]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LUXURIOUS_SPOOL
Tooltip: Power Stone Combine x9 of this item at the Thaumaturgist in the Hub to unlock the Silky power.; Older Spiders often make better quality string. It is said that female ones are even more sturdy, but it was never proven.; At 1,000 Magical Power:; +736.72&#xE007; Crit Damage +19.39&#xE022; Speed; Unique Power Bonus:; +5&#xE001; Attack Speed; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 15.; UNCOMMON POWER STONE
The Luxurious Spool is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Silky]] power for [[Accessories]], granting [[Speed]] and [[Crit Damage]]. It also provides +5 [[Attack Speed]] as its unique power bonus.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
The Luxurious Spool has a 100% chance to drop from a lower tier [[Arachne]], as well as having a 5% chance to drop from the [[Broodmother]]. It does not drop from the higher tier [[Arachne]].
## Usage
### Power Stone
The Luxurious Spool requires [[Combat|Combat XV (15)]] to be used and can only be used as a Power Stone for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9 Luxurious Spools at [[Maxwell]] will permanently unlock the Silky [[Power Stones|Power]] for [[Accessories]].
### Salvaging
Luxurious Spool can be salvaged for 1 [[Spider Essence]].
## Stats

‎
## Tips
- Due to this Power granting a lot of [[Crit Damage]], it is useful when paired with [[Mastiff Armor]], although it should be noted that the Armor set does half the damage of all Critical Hits.
- Since it grants so much [[Crit Damage]], its users should balance their Damage stats by increasing their [[Strength]] as much as possible. It synergizes well with the Withered reforge and [[Golden Dragon Pet]], both of which grants a surplus of [[Strength]].
- Its cheap price makes it extremely useful for most early game players to use after reaching 100 [[Crit Chance]].

|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Luxurious Spool to the [[Bazaar]].|

|Sep 16, 2022|Added Luxurious Spool to [[Arachne]]'s loot table.|

|Sep 28, 2022|0.15|Now gives +22.8 base [[Crit Damage]].|

# Maddox Batphone
Aliases: Batphone
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Tools, Other Tools, Slayer, Wolf Slayer

Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Wolf Slayer III (3) ([[Wolf Slayer]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 1,120x (17 stacks plus 32 (1,120 in total)) [[Cobblestone]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Iron Ingot]] 160x Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: AATROX_BATPHONE
Tooltip: Ability: Whassup? RIGHT CLICK Lets you call Maddox, when he's not busy.; ☠ Requires Wolf Slayer 3.; * Co-op Soulbound * X Rift-Transferable X UNCOMMON
Maddox Batphone is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item used to contact [[Maddox the Slayer]] remotely.
## Obtaining
### Wolf Slayer
The Maddox Batphone is a reward from reaching [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer III (3)]]. Upon reaching this milestone, the crafting recipe for this item is also unlocked.
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wolf Slayer III (3) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 1x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 7x [[Enchanted Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] ×7 + [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] + [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] → Maddox Batphone|
## Usage
Item:
Maddox Batphone
[Effects]
[Ability: Whassup? RIGHT CLICK; Lets you call Maddox (Maddox the Slayer), when he's not busy.]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Maddox Badphone]]|Vampire Slayer LVL II (2) ([[Vampire Slayer]])|[[Maddox Batphone]] 1x Maddox Batphone 8x [[Coven Seal]]|Recipe: [[Coven Seal]] x2 ×4 + Maddox Batphone → [[Maddox Badphone]]|
### Abiphone Contact
A Maddox Batphone can be given to [[Maddox the Slayer]] to get his [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Calling|✆ Ring... ✆ Ring... Ring... ✆ Ring... Ring... Ring...|
|After Three Rings|✆ Hello? [OPEN MENU]|
|After Three Rings|✆ Someone answers! [OPEN MENU]|
|After Three Rings|✆ How does a lobster answer? Shello! [OPEN MENU]|
|After Three Rings|✆ Hey what do you need? [OPEN MENU]|
|After Three Rings|✆ You hear the line pick up... [OPEN MENU]|
|After Three Rings|✆ You again? What do you want this time? [OPEN MENU]|
|On Cooldown None|✆ Please leave your message after the beep.|
|On Cooldown None|✆ How can you tell if a bee is on the phone? You get a buzzy signal!|
|On Cooldown None|✆ The phone keeps ringing, is it broken?|
|On Cooldown None|✆ The phone picks up but it immediately hangs up!|
|On Cooldown None|✆ What did the cat say on the phone? Can you hear meow?|
|On Cooldown None|✆ No answer.|
|On Cooldown None|✆ Seems like it's not picking up!|
|On Cooldown None|✆ "Your call is important to us, please stay on the line", so you hang up.|
|On Cooldown Third attempt|✆ HEY IT'S NOT PICKING UP STOP TRYING!|
|Clicking old; [OPEN MENU]|It's too late now, the phone line is off! Call again!|
|In [[Dungeons]] and The Rift ([[Rift Dimension]])|There is no reception in here!|
## Trivia
- The Maddox Batphone is used in a quest on the [[Crimson Isle]], when the player wants to join the Mage Faction.
- The internal item ID for the Maddox Batphone is "AATROX_BATPHONE", hinting that Maddox was originally called Aatrox.
  - The name Aatrox was later used to refer to the Mayoral candidate [[Aatrox]] which can appear in [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections.]]
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Oct 15, 2019|Reduced cooldown by 2 minutes, from 5 to 3 minutes.|
|Dec 3, 2019|Reduced cooldown by 2 minutes, from 3 to 1 minute.|
|Unknown Date|Reduced cooldown by 40 seconds, from 1 minute to 20 seconds.|

|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Changed crafting recipe.|

Tooltip Text: This Magical Lava Bucket holds a tiny volcano. Easy infinite lava!
Uses: Infinite Lava placement
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 30 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Netherrack]] 3x [[Iron Ingot]]
Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from merchants.): 335 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MAGICAL_LAVA_BUCKET
Tooltip: This Magical Lava Bucket holds a tiny volcano. Easy infinite lava!; UNCOMMON
The Magical Lava Bucket is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that acts like a normal [[Lava Bucket]] but never runs empty, allowing infinite [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] placement, similarly to the [[Magical Water Bucket]]. It additionally acts as an infinite fuel source (and can be taken out without getting destroyed).
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Netherrack V (5) ([[Netherrack]])|1x [[Enchanted Netherrack]] 3x [[Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] ×3 + [[Enchanted Netherrack]] → Magical Lava Bucket|
## Usage
Upon right-clicking a block on a [[Private Island]] while holding a Magical Lava Bucket, [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] will be placed at the clicked area. Unlike a normal [[Lava Bucket]], the Magical Lava Bucket will never run empty and so it requires no refilling, allowing infinite Lava placement. This is extremely helpful in building Cobblestone Generators.
Additionally, the Magical Lava Bucket can be used in a furnace as an infinite fuel (but can only be used once). However, using it as a fuel is currently bugged, as it currently will only burn for 30 minutes in a furnace.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 21, 2021|0.11.4|Added Magical Lava Bucket.|
|Nov 8, 2021|The Magical Lava Bucket can now be used as an infinite fuel source in furnaces.|

# Magical Mushroom Soup
Aliases: Soup
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Craftable items, Mushroom Collection
[Magical Mushroom Soup]
Type: Consumable item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Mushroom IV (4) ([[Mushroom]])
Tooltip Text: Consuming this Magical Mushroom Soup on your private island allows the player to fly for 2 minutes. Leaving the private island will pause the effect!
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Mystical Mushroom Soup]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 4 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 3x [[Planks]] 1x Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 1x Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 1x [[Redstone Dust]] 1x [[Glowstone Dust]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MAGIC_MUSHROOM_SOUP
Tooltip: Consuming this soup on your private island or garden allows the player to fly for 2 minutes.; Leaving the island will pause the effect!; UNCOMMON
Magical Mushroom Soup is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] consumable item unlocked in [[Mushroom|Mushroom IV (4)]] that allows players to fly on their [[Private Island]] and [[The Garden]] for 2 minutes. You can also use it while guesting someone else's Private Island or Garden.
This soup can be a cheaper flight substitute for players without a [[Booster Cookie]]. However, the flight duration is limited and you should be aware if it is about to run out and drink more preemptively.
It functions the same as [[Mystical Mushroom Soup]], giving you the ability to fly on player islands for 2 minutes instead of 200 minutes, but materials to craft it cost only 1/160th of Mystical Mushroom Soup.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Mushroom IV (4) ([[Mushroom]])|1x Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 1x [[Bowl]] 1x [[Redstone Dust]] 1x [[Glowstone Dust]] 1x Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|Recipe: [[Glowstone Dust]] + [[Mushroom]] ×2 + [[Redstone Dust]] + [[Bowl]] → Magical Mushroom Soup|
## Usage
When right-clicked, the Magical Mushroom Soup is consumed, triggering its ability.
When consumed, the Magical Mushroom Soup gives the player 2 minutes of flight only on their [[Private Island]] and also makes them invulnerable to fall damage.
When the effects are active, players will have multi-colored particles around them.
If the player leaves the island when the Magical Mushroom Soup effects are still active, the timer will pause until the player returns to the island.
The time of flight left can be extended by consuming additional soup. Warning messages will trigger when there is/are 60, 30, 10, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1 second(s) left and when the effect runs out.
## Game Message
|Condition|Random Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming|Hmm... tasty! You can now fly for 2 minutes.|
|Consuming|I feel like I can fly! You can now fly for 2 minutes.|
|Consuming|What was in that soup? You can now fly for 2 minutes.|
|Consuming when; already active|Hmm... tasty! Your flight has been extended for 2 extra minutes.|
|Consuming when; already active|I feel like I can fly! Your flight has been extended for 2 extra minutes.|
|Consuming when; already active|What was in that soup? Your flight has been extended for 2 extra minutes.|
|Effect about to expire|Mushroom Soup Flight expires in 10s Mushroom Soup Flight expires in 5s Mushroom Soup Flight expires in 4s Mushroom Soup Flight expires in 3s Mushroom Soup Flight expires in 2s Mushroom Soup Flight expires in 1s Mushroom Soup Flight has expired!|
## Trivia
- When Magical Mushroom Soup was added, there was a bug that allowed people to use the item on the [[Hub Island]]. It was fixed shortly after.
- The texture of the Magical Mushroom Soup is based on the Grub Soup from Terraria.
- The Magical Mushroom Soup used to have a different appearance and gave the Nausea effect when consumed.
  - The Nausea effect has been removed and Magical Mushroom Soup was given a different appearance and not considered a Mushroom Soup from vanilla Minecraft.
  - The custom Magical Mushroom Soup was added because the vanilla Mushroom Soup interfered with the mechanics of the soup and caused it to not stack when crafted.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 26, 2019|0.4|Added Magical Mushroom Soup.|
|Jun 29, 2019|Added a limit to how far you can fly away from your island.|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Give Nausea effect for 1 less second.|
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Flight duration is now saved when leaving private islands. Limit of one hour of flight is lifted.|
|Oct 13, 2021|Changed from an actual mushroom soup item to a custom head item, as well as changed texture from to .|
|Oct 13, 2021|Removed Nausea effect.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Magical Mushroom Soup to the [[Bazaar]].|

The Magma Slug Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Infection]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Magma Slug Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Magma Slug]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
A Magma Slug Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Crimson Isle]] (Under lava).
### Kuudra
A Magma Slug Shard can be obtained from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Loot]].
### Fusion
A Magma Slug Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Magma Slug Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Infection]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Infection ([[Attributes]]); Increases the chance of catching corrupted Sea Creatures by +0.2%–2%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Magma Slug Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Magma Slug Shard has the following description:
- Magma Slugs are highly resistant to extreme temperatures, thriving in environments where few others can survive.
## Trivia
- Despite being an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Magma Slug Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

The Magnetic Talisman is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Emerald|Emerald III (3)]]. It increases the player's item pickup range by 3x the normal range. Its effects do not stack.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Emerald III (3) ([[Emerald]])|32x [[Redstone Dust]] 128x [[Emerald]]|Recipe: [[Emerald]] x32 ×4 + [[Redstone Dust]] x32 → Magnetic Talisman|
## Trivia
- Before June 20th, 2019, the Magnetic Talisman gave the player 5x the normal range instead of 3x. This was changed due to community outrage after players abused it to 'steal' other players' drops.
- The Magnetic Talisman is a reference to the Gravity Globe from the popular game "Terraria", as they have the same appearance.
- The Magnetic Talisman's design was created by PandaClod.
- The Magnetic Talisman remains one of the only Accessories to change a fundamental part of Minecraft's vanilla mechanics.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Magnetic Talisman.|

|Vinesap VIII (8) ([[Vinesap]])|4x [[Enchanted Vinesap]] 16x [[Wet Water]] 64x [[Stretching Sticks]]|Recipe: [[Stretching Sticks]] x16 ×4 + [[Enchanted Vinesap]] ×4 + [[Wet Water]] x16 → Marshroom|
## Usage
### Reforging
Marshroom can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Marshy]] reforge, to grant [[Foraging Fortune]], [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]], which scale with the equipment's rarity. This has no [[Mining]] requirement and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece.
Additionally, it grants the Marsy Bonus, which grants [[Forest Whispers]] from Foraging.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+0.1 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +0.5 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]]) +0.2 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+0.2 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +1 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]]) +0.4 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+0.3 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +1.5 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]]) +0.6 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+0.5 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +2.5 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]]) +1 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+0.7 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +3.5 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]]) +1.4 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+0.9 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +4.5 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]]) +1.8 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+1.1 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +5.5 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]]) +2.2 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Marshy Bonus; Earn +1% Forest Whispers from foraging.|Marshy Bonus; Earn +1% Forest Whispers from foraging.|Marshy Bonus; Earn +1% Forest Whispers from foraging.|Marshy Bonus; Earn +1% Forest Whispers from foraging.|Marshy Bonus; Earn +1% Forest Whispers from foraging.|Marshy Bonus; Earn +1% Forest Whispers from foraging.|Marshy Bonus; Earn +1% Forest Whispers from foraging.|
|Cost to apply|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- As of 0.27.1, it is the only [[Reforging|Equipment Reforge]] to directly grant [[Foraging Fortune]]

Raw Materials to upgrade: 32x [[Tender Wood]] 16x [[Enchanted Fig Log]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RETIA_MELIORA
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Ability: Retentio RIGHT CLICK Traps can be placed near the spawn location of a Shard creature, with time it will eventually catch it.; Ability: Meliora Reduces the time to capture by 10%.; Ubi ars naturam superat.; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 5.; UNCOMMON TRAP
The Medium Huntrap is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Huntraps|Huntrap]], whose recipe is unlocked at [[Tender Wood|Tender Wood III (3)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Tender Wood III (3) ([[Tender Wood]])|1x [[Small Huntrap]] 16x [[Enchanted Fig Log]] 32x [[Tender Wood]]|Recipe: [[Tender Wood]] x8 ×4 + [[Enchanted Fig Log]] x4 ×4 + [[Small Huntrap]] → Medium Huntrap|
## Usage
A Medium Huntrap can be placed near the spawn location of any creature that can drop a [[Attributes|Shard]] to collect one. The time to catch a shard is random and based on the rarity of the shard caught (see [[Huntraps|Huntraps#Time]]).
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Large Huntrap]]|Tender Wood V (5) ([[Tender Wood]])|[[Medium Huntrap]] 1x Medium Huntrap 32x [[Enchanted Fig Log]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Tender Wood]]|Recipe: [[Tender Wood]] x32 ×4 + [[Enchanted Fig Log]] x8 ×4 + Medium Huntrap → [[Large Huntrap]]|
## Trivia
- The text in the Huntrap's description, "Ubi ars natura superat." is Latin, and means "Where art overcomes nature."
## Gallery
- A placed Huntrap

# Melon Juice
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Melon Juice]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Garden Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MELON_JUICE
Tooltip: Dropped rarely from Melon Slice during the Harvest Feast event.; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Melon Juice is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item dropped during the [[Harvest Feast]] used to craft [[Trunk Polish]] and [[Melon Juice Mixin]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
Melon Juice has a 0.006% (1 in 18,000 crops) chance to drop when breaking mature [[Melon|Melons]], a 15% chance to drop from a [[Earthworm]] and a 30% chance to drop from a [[Field Mouse]] while the [[Harvest Feast]] is ongoing and the crop is in season.
[[Overbloom]] is used to increase the drop chance.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Trunk Polish]]|None|1x [[Glass Bottle]] 4x [[Creambloom]] [[Melon Juice]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Melon Juice|Recipe: Melon Juice x32 ×4 + [[Creambloom]] ×4 + [[Glass Bottle]] → [[Trunk Polish]]|
|[[Melon Juice Mixin]]|None|1x [[Glass Bottle]] 2x [[Soggybud]] [[Melon Juice]] 16x Melon Juice|Recipe: Melon Juice x16 + [[Soggybud]] x2 + [[Glass Bottle]] → [[Melon Juice Mixin]]|
### [[The Garden|Garden Visitors]]
When visiting [[The Garden]], [[Taylor]] can request [[Melon Juice]] 256 (4 stacks) Melon Juice in exchange for the [[Greenhouse Skins|Jelly Garden Greenhouse Skin]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 31, 2026|Added Melon Juice.|
|Jul 15, 2026|Changed NPC Sell Price from 5,000 [[Coins]] to 50,000 [[Coins]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Added Melon Juice.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed NPC Sell Price from 5,000 [[Coins]] to 50,000 [[Coins]].|

The Miniaturized Tubulator is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
The Miniaturized Tubulator can be claimed after completing the seventh puzzle in the [[Mirrorverse]] without using checkpoints.
## Usage
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], the Miniaturized Tubulator can be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]].
## Trivia
- The Miniaturized Tubulator's description is most likely a reference to a famous quote from the 1999 Ice-T song "Don't Hate the Playa".

The Minos Hunter Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Mythological Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Minos Hunter Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Minos Hunter]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Minos Hunter Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Minos Hunter Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Mythological Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Mythological Ruler ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3%–30% more [[Damage]] against [[Mythological]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

The Mithril Cloak is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Cloaks|Cloak]] obtained through Forging. It is part of the [[Mithril Equipment]] set.
## Obtaining
The Mithril Cloak can be forged in [[The Forge]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It takes 1 hour to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Mithril Cloak]] 1 Mithril Cloak
  - 3 [[Enchanted Mithril]] 480 (7 stacks plus 32) [[Mithril]]

# Mithril Infusion
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Craftable items, Mithril Collection, Minion Modifiers
[Mithril Infusion]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Mithril VIII (8) ([[Mithril]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Shop]
Sell: 4,346 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 102,400x (1,600 stacks (102,400 items total)) [[Coal]] 81,920x (1,280 stacks (81,920 items total)) [[Mithril]] 960x (15 stacks (960 items total)) [[Iron Ingot]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MITHRIL_INFUSION
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; When applied to minions, permanently increases minion speed by 10%.; UNCOMMON
A Mithril Infusion is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Mithril VIII (8) ([[Mithril]])|1x [[Magma Bucket]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Enchanted Mithril]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mithril]] x64 ×8 + [[Magma Bucket]] → Mithril Infusion|
## Usage
Mithril Infusion can be used to boost all [[Minions]]. It can be applied in an [[Anvil]] or directly in the [[Minions|Minion]]'s menu. It permanently increases the [[Minions|Minion]]'s speed by 10%.
## Bugs
- Applying a Mithril Infusion in an [[Anvil]] costs 20 [[Experience]] Levels, while applying it directly in the [[Minions|Minion]]'s menu does not cost any [[Experience]] Levels.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MITHRIL_LANTERN
Tooltip: Mining Deployable; Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK Place an orb for 5m buffing up to 5; players within 30 blocks.; Costs 50% of max mana.; Orb Buff: Mithril Lantern ▪ Grants +40 Mining Speed.; ▪ Grants +10 Mining Fortune.; Only one deployable buff applies.; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 2.; UNCOMMON DEPLOYABLE
A Mithril Lantern is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Deployables|Deployable]].
## Obtaining
### Forge
A Mithril Lantern can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It takes 30 minutes to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Mithril Lantern]] 1 Mithril Lantern
  - 1 [[Dwarven Lantern]]
  - 1 [[Refined Mithril]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Mithril]]
  - 16 [[Enchanted Emerald]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Emerald]]
## Usage
Using the ability of a Mithril Lantern costs 50% of player's max [[Mana]], this can be reduced down to 40% using [[Mana Disintegrator|Mana Disintegrators]]. Casting the ability grants up to 5 players in 30 block radius the following stats:
- +40 [[Mining Speed]]
- +10 [[Mining Fortune]]
When used alongside multiple or different lantern deployables, only one deployable effect applies and only the one of higher rarity will apply its effects.
### [[The Forge|Forging]] Recipe
|Slot|Name|HotM ([[Heart of the Mountain]]); Requirements|Forging ([[The Forge]]) recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Titanium Lantern]]|[[Titanium Lantern]]|5|1 [[Titanium Lantern]] [[Mithril Lantern]] 1 Mithril Lantern 1 [[Dwarven Lantern]] 1 [[Refined Mithril]] 160 [[Enchanted Mithril]] 25,600 [[Mithril]] 16 [[Enchanted Emerald]] 2,560 [[Emerald]] 2 [[Refined Titanium]] 32 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 5,120 (80 stacks) [[Titanium]] 1 [[Enchanted Emerald Block]] 160 [[Enchanted Emerald]] 25,600 [[Emerald]]|

Tooltip: Strong Legs I Your Axe gains +3  Sweep on melee.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 5.; UNCOMMON FOREST SHARD (ID U31)
The Mochibear Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Strong Legs]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Mochibear Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Mochibear]].
### Huntraps
A Mochibear Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Tranquility Sanctum]] (Autumn or Winter).
### Fusion
A Mochibear Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Mochibear Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Strong Legs]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Strong Legs ([[Attributes]]); Your Axe gains +3–30 [[Sweep]] on melee.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Mochibear Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Mochibear Shard has the following description:
- Its thick, velvety fur not only provides warmth but also gives Mochibear a cuddly appearance.

Buy: 50,000 [[Coins]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MOIL_LOG
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Moil reforge when combined with an Axe.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Foraging Fortune; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 5.; UNCOMMON REFORGE STONE
The Moil Log is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Moil]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to an [[Axes|Axe]], which grants [[Foraging Fortune]].
## Obtaining
The Moil Log can be purchased from the [[Smithmonger]] for 50,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Moil Log can be combined with an [[Axes|Axe]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Moil]] reforge, to grant [[Foraging Fortune]], which scales with the Axe's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining V (5)]]. An Axe that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Moil]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+1 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|+2 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|+3 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|+4 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|+6 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|+8 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|+10 For.Frt ([[Foraging Fortune]])|
|Cost to apply|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|

## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Mosquito Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Mosquito]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Mosquito Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Mosquito Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Enchanted Farmer]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Enchanted Farmer ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +0.001%–0.01% chance to find an Enchanted Crop while Farming.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

The Mutations Sack is a [[Sacks|Sack]] unlocked from the [[Crop Analyzer]] [[Crop Analyzer|DNA Analysis Milestone I (1)]], [[Crop Analyzer|DNA Analysis Milestone III (3)]], and [[Crop Analyzer|DNA Analysis Milestone V (5)]] with increasing capacity. The Small Sack can hold 64 of each item, the Medium Sack can hold 256 of each item, and the Large Sack can hold 1,024 of each item.
## Obtaining
A Small Sack is given to the player upon reaching [[Crop Analyzer|DNA Analysis Milestone I (1)]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])|DNA Analysis Milestone I (1) ([[Crop Analyzer]])|1x [[Enchanted Leather]] 4x [[Dustgrain]] 4x [[Veilshroom]]|Recipe: [[Veilshroom]] ×4 + [[Dustgrain]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Leather]] → [[Mutations Sack]]|
|Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])|DNA Analysis Milestone III (3) ([[Crop Analyzer]])|1x Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]) 4x [[Coalroot]] 4x [[Creambloom]] 8x [[Chocoberry]]|Recipe: [[Chocoberry]] x2 ×4 + [[Creambloom]] x2 ×2 + [[Coalroot]] x2 ×2 + [[Mutations Sack]] → [[Mutations Sack]]|
|Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])|DNA Analysis Milestone V (5) ([[Crop Analyzer]])|1x Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]) 8x [[Cheesebite]] 8x [[Blastberry]] 16x [[Fleshtrap]]|Recipe: [[Fleshtrap]] x4 ×4 + [[Blastberry]] x4 ×2 + [[Cheesebite]] x4 ×2 + [[Mutations Sack]] → [[Mutations Sack]]|
## Usage
The Mutations Sack can store [[Mutations]].
|Sack|Capacity|Items|

Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Mob Drops
[Muted Bark]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MUTED_BARK
Tooltip: No longer barking.; UNCOMMON
Muted Bark is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv2]|[[Barking Wolf]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
## Usage
Clicking on the log in [[The Bastion (Rift)|The Bastion]] surrounded by particles at [[Coordinates|-15, 91, 94]] with different amounts of Muted Bark will trigger different messages. Holding [[Muted Bark]] 20 Muted Bark and interacting with the log will remove it and reveal an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]].
### Game Messages
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Without Muted Bark|You knock on wood... Everything stays the same!|
|With less than [[Muted Bark]] 20 Muted Bark|You knock on wood... Try with more bark!|
|With [[Muted Bark]] 20 Muted Bark|You knock on wood... Its opacity turned to 0! (It became muted...)|
## Trivia
- This item and Enigma Soul associated with it are a play on words relating to the idiom "You're barking at the wrong tree".
## History

# New Year Cake Bag
Aliases: Cake Bag, Cake bag
Categories: Uncommon items, Accessories, Events, New Year Celebration, Container, Cake
[New Year Cake Bag]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Baker]]
Buy: 250,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NEW_YEAR_CAKE_BAG
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; Holds New Year Cakes. Each unique New Year Cake within this bag grants +1 Health.; Current Bonus: +0 Health; Right-click to open!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The New Year Cake Bag is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] purchasable from the [[Baker]] during the [[New Year Celebration]] for 250,000 [[Coins]].
## Description
The New Year Cake Bag holds [[New Year Cake|New Year Cakes]]. Each unique New Year Cake in the bag grants the player +1 [[Health]], with the bag being able to hold up to 54 total cakes. The effect does not stack.
The New Year Cake is considered unique if there is only one cake per year. For example, two cakes obtained during Year 70 will only grant +1 [[Health]] instead of +2 .
## Notes
- Having multiple New Year Cake Bags in [[Accessory Bag]], will count the [[Health]] bonus from the first one only, regardless of if there is a stronger one.
## History

|Apr 8, 2021|The New Year Cake Bag has been re-enabled|
|Apr 8, 2021|New Year Cake Bag has been temporarily disabled, again.|
|Jun 1, 2021|The New Year Cake Bag has been re-enabled.|

# Night Saver
Categories: Uncommon items, Blocks, Craftable items, Pages with UIs, Nether Quartz Collection, Time Saver
[Night Saver]
Type: Block
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Nether Quartz II (2) ([[Nether Quartz]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 35 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 3x [[Sand]] 15x [[Nether Quartz]] 3x Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NIGHT_SAVER
Tooltip: Control the time on your private island when paired with a Day Saver.; UNCOMMON
The Night Saver is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] block that is unlocked at [[Nether Quartz|Nether Quartz II (2)]]. It can be used to unlock the Island Time menu on a [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]] to make it always night time on the island.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Nether Quartz II (2) ([[Nether Quartz]])|3x Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 3x [[Glass]] 15x [[Nether Quartz]]|Recipe: [[Glass]] ×3 + [[Nether Quartz]] x5 ×3 + [[Mushroom]] ×3 → Night Saver|
|Nether Quartz II (2) ([[Nether Quartz]])|3x [[Glass]] 3x Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) 15x [[Nether Quartz]]|Recipe: [[Glass]] ×3 + [[Nether Quartz]] x5 ×3 + [[Mushroom]] ×3 → Night Saver|
## Usage
A [[Day Saver]] followed by a Night Saver can be used to unlock the Island Time menu on a [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]], after which there will be an option to set the time in one hour increments, as well as whether to freeze the time.
It allows mobs that generate naturally at night to spawn constantly. This can be used to farm [[Bat (disambiguation)|Bats]] more efficiently.
## Interface
The Night Saver originally featured its own interface when placed down. However, after the 0.19.9 update, this was merged with the [[Day Saver]] and replaced by the Island Time menu found under the Island Properties menu on the [[Private Island]] or within the [[The Garden|Desk]] inside [[The Garden]]
## Chat Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Placed on [[Private Island]]; After 0.19.9 Update|You can't place this! Unlock this feature in the Island Properties menu. CLICK HERE|
|Placed on [[The Garden]]; After 0.19.9 Update|You can't place this! Unlock this feature in the Desk menu. CLICK HERE|
|Placed on [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]]; Before 0.19.9 Update|You placed a Night Saver! It will now always be night! RIGHT CLICK the Night Saver to change its settings!|
|Setting time; Before 0.19.9 Update|Your Night Saver's time has been set to time!|
|Attempting to place with a [[Day Saver]] already present; Before 0.19.9 Update|You can only place 1 of these on your island at a time!|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 26, 2019|0.4|Added Night Saver.|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|The Night Saver can select the exact time to set when right-clicked.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Limited the Night Savers to only 1 on private islands.|
|Dec 12, 2023|0.19.9|Changed Night Saver from a placed block to a [[Settings]] feature.|

A Null Sphere is a [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item. It is an [[Drop Chance|1Guaranteed]] drop used to craft many items related to [[Enderman Slayer]].
## Obtaining
### Voidgloom Seraph
Null Spheres are [[Drop Chance|1Guaranteed]] drops from all tiers of the [[Voidgloom Seraph]]. The amount dropped varies based on the tier of the boss.
- Tier I: 2-3x
- Tier II: 14-24x
- Tier III: 60-80x
- Tier IV: 105-155x

|[Lv373]|[[Voidling Devotee]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|

|[[Voidwalker Katana]]|Enderman Slayer I (1) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x [[Stick]] [[Null Sphere]] 32x Null Sphere|Recipe: Null Sphere x16 ×2 + [[Stick]] → [[Voidwalker Katana]]|

|Voidling Minion I ([[Voidling Minion]])|Enderman Slayer IV (4) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x Enderman Minion I ([[Enderman Minion]]) [[Null Sphere]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Null Sphere|Recipe: Null Sphere x10 ×8 + [[Enderman Minion]] → [[Voidling Minion]]|
|Voidling Minion II ([[Voidling Minion]])|Enderman Slayer IV (4) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x Obsidian Minion I ([[Obsidian Minion]]) 1x Voidling Minion I ([[Voidling Minion]]) [[Null Sphere]] 140x (2 stacks plus 12 (140 in total)) Null Sphere|Recipe: Null Sphere x20 ×7 + [[Voidling Minion]] + [[Obsidian Minion]] → [[Voidling Minion]]|
|Voidling Minion III ([[Voidling Minion]])|Enderman Slayer IV (4) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x Enderman Minion II ([[Enderman Minion]]) 1x Voidling Minion II ([[Voidling Minion]]) [[Null Sphere]] 280x (4 stacks plus 24 (280 in total)) Null Sphere|Recipe: Null Sphere x40 ×7 + [[Voidling Minion]] + [[Enderman Minion]] → [[Voidling Minion]]|

[Metadata]

Shard Item ID: OBSIDIAN_DEFENDER
Tooltip: Speed I Grants +1  Speed.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; UNCOMMON COMBAT SHARD (ID U18)
The Obsidian Defender Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Speed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
An Obsidian Defender Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] an [[Obsidian Defender]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
An Obsidian Defender Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Dragon's Nest]], near where [[Obsidian Defender|Obsidian Defenders]] spawn.
### Fusion
An Obsidian Defender Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Obsidian Defender Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Speed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Speed ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Speed]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
An Obsidian Defender Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Obsidian Defender Shard has the following description:
- These formidable guardians remain still until provoked, then spring into action with incredible swiftness and strength.
## Trivia
- Despite being an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Obsidian Defender Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

# Oil Barrel
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crystal Hollows, Machine Fuels
[Oil Barrel]
Type: [[Machine Fuel]]
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Large Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); [[Dwarven Sack]]; [[Crystal Hollows Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: OIL_BARREL
Tooltip: Machine Fuel; Adds +10,000 Fuel to a refuelable machine.; 100% unrenewable energy!; UNCOMMON
The Oil Barrel is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Oil Barrels can be obtained from treasure chests in the [[Crystal Hollows]] or as a reward from placing Crystals in the [[Crystal Nucleus]].
## Usage
The Oil Barrel grants 10,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]], and can be applied in a [[Drill Anvil]] with any [[Drills|Drill]] to give it fuel. Additionally, it can be placed in the [[Composter]] to add 10k [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] to the Fuel Meter.
## Trivia
- The Oil Barrel's description "100% unrenewable energy!" is a reference to the [[Biofuel]]'s description "100% renewable energy".
## History

|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Oil Barrel to the [[Bazaar]].|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: OVERFLOWING_TRASH_CAN
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Trashy reforge when combined with a Fishing Rod.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Fishing Speed  Sea Creature Chance; Trashy Bonus Grants +1 Treasure Chance while in the  Backwater Bayou.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 3.; UNCOMMON REFORGE STONE
An Overflowing Trash Can is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Trashy]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], which grants [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv21]|[[Dumpster Diver]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|
## Usage
### Reforging
Overflowing Trash Can can be applied to [[Fishing Rods]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Trashy]] reforge, to grant [[Fishing Speed]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]], which scale with the Fishing Rod's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining III (3)]]. A Fishing Rod that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Trashy]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Trashy Bonus, which grants 1 [[Treasure Chance]] while on the [[Backwater Bayou]].
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+0.5 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +1 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+1 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +2 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+1.5 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +3 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+2 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +4 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+3 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +6 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+4 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +8 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+5 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +10 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Trashy Bonus; Grants +1 Treasure Chance while in the Backwater Bayou.|Trashy Bonus; Grants +1 Treasure Chance while in the Backwater Bayou.|Trashy Bonus; Grants +1 Treasure Chance while in the Backwater Bayou.|Trashy Bonus; Grants +1 Treasure Chance while in the Backwater Bayou.|Trashy Bonus; Grants +1 Treasure Chance while in the Backwater Bayou.|Trashy Bonus; Grants +1 Treasure Chance while in the Backwater Bayou.|Trashy Bonus; Grants +1 Treasure Chance while in the Backwater Bayou.|
|Cost to apply|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|
