# Personal Compactor 4000
Categories: Uncommon items, Accessories, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Redstone Dust Collection
[Personal Compactor 4000]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Redstone Dust VI (6) ([[Redstone Dust]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Personal Compactor 5000]]
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 9,760x (152 stacks plus 32 (9,760 in total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]]) 36,960x (577 stacks plus 32 (36,960 in total)) [[Cobblestone]]
Raw Materials to upgrade: 4,480x (70 stacks (4,480 items total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PERSONAL_COMPACTOR_4000
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; Automatically turns certain materials in your inventory into their enchanted form.; Right-click to configure!; Does not grant Carpentry XP!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The Personal Compactor 4000 is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Redstone Dust|Redstone Dust VI (6)]]. Its ability automatically converts certain materials in the inventory into their enchanted form, similar to the [[Super Compactor 3000]]. It saves time crafting when grinding large quantities of items, like Wood, Crops, or [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]].
Unlike the Super Compactor, the user needs the recipe for the items it turns to its respective Enchanted form. Unlike regular crafting, using Personal Compactors does not grant [[Carpentry]] experience from crafting enchanted items.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Redstone Dust VI (6) ([[Redstone Dust]])|1x [[Super Compactor 3000]] 28x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] x4 ×7 + [[Super Compactor 3000]] → Personal Compactor 4000|
## Usage
Right-clicking the accessory while in the hand will open its configuration menu, where the player can place one enchanted item in the Auto-Crafting slot. When there are enough items in the player's inventory, it will automatically convert them into their enchanted form. Selecting an item to be converted does not remove it from player's inventory and is merely used as a visual indicator. It has only 1 slot.
Besides for strictly enchanted items, Personal Compactor can also turn certain items into their more compacted, but not enchanted form. Examples of this include turning iron, diamonds, coal, glowstone dust, wheat, melon slices etc. to their corresponding blocks. Exceptions include for example turning prismarine shards or strings into prismarine blocks or cobwebs, respectively.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Personal Compactor 5000]]|Redstone Dust VII (7) ([[Redstone Dust]])|[[Personal Compactor 4000]] 1x Personal Compactor 4000 84x (1 stack plus 20 (84 in total)) [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] x12 ×7 + Personal Compactor 4000 → [[Personal Compactor 5000]]|
## Tips
This accessory by itself is considered to be extremely useful for players who:
- Have a massive farm with one/few crops
- Grind mobs whos drops have a high stack count (i.e. zombies, skeletons, etc.)
- Grind enchanted blocks like: [[Enchanted Oak Log|Enchanted Oak Wood]], [[Enchanted Cobblestone]], [[Enchanted Ender Pearl|Enchanted Ender Pearls]], etc.
Players should consider upgrading if they:
- Plan on grinding many different things in a short time frame
- Grind something who's drops have a low stack count (i.e. Endermen)
- Have spare enchanted Redstone
- Can afford it
Players should not consider upgrading if they:
- Cannot afford it
- Do not need the extra slots immediately
- Are unable to go up all the tiers at once
## Trivia
- This was a leaked item in discord by former admin 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Minikloon.
- This is one of the few accessories that passes its reforges to its upgraded forms.
- [[Experience Artifact]], Personal Compactor, [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]], [[Book of Progression]], [[Melody's Hair]], [[Zombie Talisman]], [[Skeleton Talisman]], [[Archaeologist's Compass]], [[Lucky Hoof]], [[Eternal Hoof]], [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]] and [[Ring of Love|Yellow Rock of Love]] are the only accessories to use a vanilla sprite, rather than a custom made one.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 7, 2020|0.7.10|Added Personal Compactor 4000.|
|Jun 7, 2020|0.7.10|Updated recipe to require a [[Super Compactor 3000]] instead of a [[Compactor]].|
|Jun 10, 2020|Now requires 7 Enchanted Redstone Blocks ([[Enchanted Redstone Block]]) to craft, from 14.|
|Unknown Date|No longer gives [[Carpentry]] xp.|
|Sep 21, 2021|Items placed in the Personal Compactor 4000 are no longer placed inside, but a duplicate, unusable item is placed inside.|
|Aug 25, 2023|Fixed adding the same items to Personal Compactor/Deletor.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Changed Crafting Recipe from 7 [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] and 1 [[Super Compactor 3000]] to 448 (7 stacks) [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] and 1 [[Super Compactor 3000]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed Crafting Recipe from 448 [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] and 1 [[Super Compactor 3000]] to 28 [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] and 1 [[Super Compactor 3000]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed crafting requirement from Redstone Dust IX (9) ([[Redstone Dust]]) to Redstone Dust VI (6) ([[Redstone Dust]]).|

# Personal Deletor 4000
Aliases: Deletor, Deletors (disambiguation), Personal Deletor, Personal Deletors, Personal Deletors (disambiguation)
Categories: Uncommon items, Accessories, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Iron Ingot Collection
[Personal Deletor 4000]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Iron Ingot IX (9) ([[Iron Ingot]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Personal Deletor 5000]]
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 18,400x (287 stacks plus 32 (18,400 in total)) [[Iron Ingot]] 51,200x (800 stacks (51,200 items total)) [[Coal]] 25,600x (400 stacks (25,600 items total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PERSONAL_DELETOR_4000
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; Automatically delete specific items on pickup.; Right-click to configure!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The Personal Deletor 4000 is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingot IX (9)]]. Its ability automatically deletes certain materials in the inventory when picked up. It can be upgraded to the [[Personal Deletor 5000]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Iron Ingot IX (9) ([[Iron Ingot]])|1x [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 1x [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]] 112x (1 stack plus 48 (112 in total)) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x16 ×7 + [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]] + [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] → Personal Deletor 4000|
## Usage
Right-clicking the accessory while in the hand will open its configuration menu, where the player can place one item in the Auto-Deleting slot, similar to the [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactors]]. When the respective item enters the player's inventory, it will get deleted. It has only 1 slot. It also has a switch that must be turned on in order for this device to function.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Personal Deletor 5000]]|Iron Ingot X (10) ([[Iron Ingot]])|[[Personal Deletor 4000]] 1x Personal Deletor 4000 224x (3 stacks plus 32 (224 in total)) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x32 ×7 + Personal Deletor 4000 → [[Personal Deletor 5000]]|
## Trivia
- The [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]] will only remove items from your inventory that you pick up off the ground. This is regardless of the [[Rarity]] of the item. However, it will not delete any item that does not have the same SkyBlock ID of the item in the deletor.
  - Example: You obtain a personal deletor and place a [[Golden Sword]] inside, any item you pick up with the SkyBlock ID of GOLDEN_SWORD will be deleted. The [[Midas' Sword]], which uses the Golden Sword item for its texture, will not be deleted upon pickup, as it does not have the SkyBlock ID of GOLDEN_SWORD.
- Unlike mentioned previously, when the Personal Deletors were first released, if players placed a vanilla iron sword in the GUI, it would also cause every in-game item with an iron sword texture (e.g. the [[Giant's Sword]] and [[Hyperion]]) to be deleted. This led to the Deletor being temporarily disabled.
  - However, certain items that share the same SkyBlock ID will still get deleted. For example, since all potions have a SkyBlock ID of POTION, putting any potion in a personal deletor would result in all potions being deleted.
    - This was partially patched in the 0.19.10 update by preventing potions from being placed in Personal Deletors.
- Secrets could be deleted without receiving credit for finding them, by putting a Secret item like a [[Decoy]] in a Personal Deletor and attempting to pick up the item.
  - This was later patched.
- [[Experience Artifact]], [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactor]], Personal Deletor, [[Book of Progression]], [[Melody's Hair]], [[Zombie Talisman]], [[Skeleton Talisman]], [[Archaeologist's Compass]], [[Lucky Hoof]], [[Eternal Hoof]], [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]] and [[Ring of Love|Yellow Rock of Love]] are the only accessories to use a vanilla sprite, rather than a custom made one.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Mar 9, 2021|0.11.2|Added Personal Deletor 4000.|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|Fixed Personal Deletor not working on loots from Frozen Treasures ([[Glacial Cave]]).|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed the Personal Deletor not deleting double catch items from [[Fishing]].|
|Aug 25, 2023|Fixed adding the same items to Personal Compactor/Deletor.|
|Jan 16, 2024|0.19.10|Potions can no longer be placed in Personal Deletors.|

# Pest Repellent
Aliases: Pest Repellant, Pest Spray, Repellent
Categories: Uncommon items, Items

Type: Item

[Tiers]

Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PEST_REPELLENT
Tooltip: Consumable; Consume to cover yourself in a pleasant aroma that  Pests find truly revolting for 60m.; While active,  Pests will take 2x as long to spawn.; Right-click to consume!; UNCOMMON
Pest Repellent is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Pest Repellent can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 15 [[Copper]].
## Usage
Pest Repellent can be consumed to reduce [[Pests|Pest]] spawn rates by 2x for 60 minutes. It does not reduce [[Bonus Pest Chance]].
Upon consuming, it gives the player the Pest Repellent I potion effect.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming|YUM! Pests will now spawn 2x less while you break crops for the next 60m!|
|Attempting to consume when Pest Repellent I or II is already active|You already have this effect active! Try again in #m #s!|
## Tips
- Since you cannot consume another Pest Repellent while one is already active, it is recommended to consume one at the beginning of each hour if you plan to participate in Jacob's Contest. If you consume one right before or during a contest, you will likely have to stop for a few seconds in the middle of the next contest to consume another one when it expires.
  - You can consume a [[Pest Repellent MAX]] while a regular Pest Repellent is already active and the timer will reset to 60 minutes. However, if a [[Pest Repellent MAX]] is active, you cannot consume either one again until it expires.
  - You can drink a [[Milk Bucket]] to clear the effect early and be able to consume another one, however this will also clear all other active potion effects, including the [[God Potion]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Nov 9, 2023|Added Pest Spray.|
|Nov 9, 2023|Added functionality to Pest Spray.|
|Nov 11, 2023|Renamed to Pest Repellent.|
|Nov 11, 2023|Changed cost from 50,000 [[Coins]] to 15 [[Copper]].|

# Pest Vinyls
Aliases: Pest Vinyl, Vinyl, Vinyls
Categories: Uncommon items, Items
[Pest Vinyls]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Pests]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VINYL_BEETLE; VINYL_CRICKET_CHOIR; VINYL_IMAGINE_DRAGONFLIES; VINYL_EARTHWORM_ENSEMBLE; VINYL_FIREFLY; VINYL_PRETTY_FLY; VINYL_CICADA_SYMPHONY; VINYL_DYNAMITES; VINYL_BUZZIN_BEATS; VINYL_WINGS_OF_HARMONY; VINYL_PRAY_FOR_ME; VINYL_RODENT_REVOLUTION; VINYL_SLOW_AND_GROOVY
Tooltip: Place in a Chirping Stereo to play funky tunes for  Pests.; When playing, this vinyl attracts Beetle Pests.; UNCOMMON
Pest Vinyls, often shortened to Vinyls, are [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] items dropped from [[Pests]] that can be played in [[InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius]] using its Stereo Harmony ability to increase the spawn rate of its respective pest.
## Obtaining
### Pests
All Pest Vinyls have an associated [[Pests|Pest]], from which they are dropped. The chance to drop a Pest Vinyl is the same for them all, and can be increased with [[Overbloom]].
|Name|Vinyl|Chance|
|-|-|-|
|Beetle|[[Not Just a Pest Vinyl]]|5%|
|Cricket|[[Cricket Choir Vinyl]]|5%|
|Dragonfly|[[Imagine Dragonflies Vinyl]]|5%|
|Earthworm|[[Earthworm Ensemble Vinyl]]|5%|
|Firefly|[[Firefly in the Hole Vinyl]]|5%|
|Fly|[[Pretty Fly Vinyl]]|5%|
|Locust|[[Cicada Symphony Vinyl]]|5%|
|Mite|[[DynaMITES Vinyl]]|5%|
|Mosquito|[[Buzzin' Beats Vinyl]]|5%|
|Moth|[[Wings of Harmony Vinyl]]|5%|
|Praying Mantis|[[Pray For Me Vinyl]]|5%|
|Rat|[[Rodent Revolution Vinyl]]|5%|
|Slug|[[Slow and Groovy Vinyl]]|5%|
## Usage
### Stereo Harmony
Pest Vinyls can be inserted into the [[InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius]] to grant the player the ability to play them, making the selected Pest more likely to appear. The Smooth Jazz ability from [[Mosquito Pet]] increases the effectiveness of Stereo Harmony
### Vinyl Collector
When [[Finnegan]]'s Blooming Business perk is active, [[Vinyl Collector]] can visit [[The Garden]], where they will ask for 1 randomly selected Pest Vinyl.
### Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set
Any 5 Pest Vinyls can be placed in a [[Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set]] and gifted to a [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]] to charm them, ensuring they will have better rewards on their next visit.
### Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination
Can be filled with any Pest Vinyl. When a specific Pest Vinyl is inserted, its corresponding [[Pests|Pest]] will spawn within 10 blocks of its location when appearing on [[The Garden]]. This only applies to pests that spawn when [[The Garden]] isn't loaded.
## Notes
- The [[Attributes|Groovy Radar]] attribute grants the player a 25% (1 in 4) chance for a pest to drop a second Vinyl. With this attribute, players will get, on average, 0.0625 Vinyls per pest slain (excluding [[Overbloom]].)
## Tips
- To maximize the chance for a specific pest to spawn, this item is best used with one of the [[Sprayonators]].
- Pest Vinyls only affect the chances of spawning certain pests, not the amount of pests that spawn at a time. For that, players should aim to increase their [[Bonus Pest Chance]] stat.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Nov 8, 2023|Added Pest Vinyls.|
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Imagine Dragonflies Vinyl, Firefly in the Hole Vinyl, and Pray For Me Vinyl.|
|Mar 31, 2026|Changed the appearance of the following vinyls: [[Imagine Dragonflies Vinyl]], [[Firefly in the Hole Vinyl]], and [[Pray For Me Vinyl]].|
|Oct 15, 2026|Pest Vinyls are now stackable.|
|Oct 15, 2026|Added [[Vinyl Sack]] to store Pest Vinyls.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 14, 2023|0.19.7|Added Pest Vinyls.|
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Imagine Dragonflies Vinyl, Firefly in the Hole Vinyl, and Pray For Me Vinyl.|
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Changed the appearance of the following vinyls: [[Imagine Dragonflies Vinyl]], [[Firefly in the Hole Vinyl]], and [[Pray For Me Vinyl]].|

[Item Properties]

[Shop]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BROKEN_PIGGY_BANK
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; Saves your coins from death.; Only when in inventory or Accessory Bag.; Fragile!; Triggers when losing 20k+ coins.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The Piggy Bank is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that allows the player to keep their [[Coins]] upon death. It is unlocked at [[Raw Porkchop|Raw Porkchop V (5)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Porkchop V (5) ([[Raw Porkchop]])|1x [[Chest]] 40x [[Enchanted Raw Porkchop]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Raw Porkchop]] x5 ×8 + [[Chest]] → Piggy Bank|
## Usage
### Breaking
This effect only works when the piggy is held in the player's inventory or in the player's accessory bag and results in the Piggy Bank becoming a Cracked Piggy Bank after being. The Cracked Piggy Bank saves only 75% of the player's [[Coins]] when they die. Upon a second death, the Cracked Piggy Bank will become a Broken Piggy Bank and will no longer save [[Coins]] but will still give 5 [[Accessory Power]]. The ability only triggers when losing over 20,000 [[Coins]]. When the coin amount is lower than 20,000 [[Coins]], then the player will lose half of their [[Coins]], and the Piggy Bank will not be affected by the death.
If at least two armor pieces the player is wearing are enchanted with [[Bank (Enchantment)|Bank V]], the Piggy Bank will only ever become cracked, not broken.
|Condition|Image|Repairable|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|Normal|Piggy Bank|No|Saves all your coins from death. Fragile!|
|Cracked|Cracked Piggy Bank|Yes|Saves 75% of your coins on death. Very fragile!|
|Broken|Broken Piggy Bank|Yes|None, but it is still worth the same [[Accessory Power]]|
### Repairing
A Cracked or Broken Piggy Bank can be repaired with 8 [[Enchanted Raw Porkchop]]. The Piggy Bank recipe doesn't need to be unlocked.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|Cracked Piggy Bank ([[Piggy Bank]]) or Broken Piggy Bank ([[Piggy Bank]]) 8 [[Enchanted Raw Porkchop]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Raw Porkchop]] ×8 + [[Piggy Bank]] → Piggy Bank|
## Trivia
- Having a Piggy Bank causes a change in the text in your sidebar, replacing "[[Purse]]:" with "Piggy:".
- Until Version 0.7.45, the Piggy Bank allowed players to deposit coins into their [[Bank]] on right-click, similar to the [[Personal Bank]]. There was a bug in which after depositing coins in the Piggy Bank, it would open the [[Bank]] menu, allowing you to withdraw coins as well. This bug was fixed on Dec 2, 2019, shortly before the functionality of the item was overhauled.
- Normal, Cracked and Broken Piggy Banks used to count as different accessories and allowed players to get 3 different reforges on them. This has been patched in an update.
- Piggy Banks were supposedly invented by [[Diaz]]'s father.
## Demonstration
Piggy Bank Demonstration
## History

|Dec 3, 2019|Piggy Bank functionality has been overhauled. It now saves your coins on death.|
|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Fixed cracked Piggy Bank description, 75% of coins are saved, not 25%.|
|May 21, 2020|Fixed Piggy Bank stacking (all count as same accessory).|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Piggy Bank now works in the [[Accessory Bag]].|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|Piggy Bank now displays as working in the Accessory Bag. The tooltip changed from "Only when in player inventory" to "Only when in player inventory or Accessory Bag".|

Tooltip: Can be spread across a Garden plot with a Sprayonator or used in Pest Traps.; Attracts: Locust, Slug; UNCOMMON
Plant Matter is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can be used with a [[Sprayonator]] to attract [[Pests]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Field Mouse]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv1]|[[Beetle]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Cricket]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Dragonfly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Earthworm]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Firefly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Fly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Locust]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Mite]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Mosquito]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Moth]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Praying Mantis]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Rat]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Slug]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
### Farming
A Plant Matter can also be obtained from the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Orchid Mantis Pet]]'s Orchid Nectar perk, up to a 0.02% (1 in 5000) chance when farming crops.
### Attributes
[[Attributes|Visitor Plant]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants up to 2.5% chance for [[Garden Visitors|Visitors]] to bring Plant Matter, which can be further increased with [[Attributes|Queenly Echo]] attribute.
[[Attributes|Sprayonator Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants 10% chance for [[Pests]] to drop an extra [[Sprayonator|Sprayonator Material]].
## Usage
Plant Matter can be used with a [[Sprayonator]] to attract [[Locust|Locusts]] and [[Slug|Slugs]] to a sprayed plot.
Plant Matter can also be used as bait in a [[Pest Trap]], [[Mouse Trap]] or [[Vermin Trap]], allowing them to catch [[Pests]], and increasing the chance to catch a [[Locust]] or a [[Slug]] by 12x.
It can be also used as [[Machine Fuel]], adding 150 Fuel.

## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Plumber's Sponge can be purchased from [[Plumber Joe]] for 50 [[Coins]].
## Usage
A Plumber's Sponge can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] placed on [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|water]] or [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|lava]] to remove all of the connected fluid type up to 30 blocks away. It disappears upon use.

The Polaris Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Frozen Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Polaris Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Polaris]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Polaris Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Polaris Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Frozen Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Frozen Ruler ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3%–30% more [[Damage]] against Frozen ([[Frozen (Mob Type)]]) mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|

# Premium Flesh
Categories: Uncommon items, Reforge stones, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mob Drops
The Zombie it came from was probably a great warrior before its death.
[Premium Flesh]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: The Zombie it came from was probably a great warrior before its death.
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Undead
Applied To: [[Armor]]
Apply Cost: 25,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XVI (16) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PREMIUM_FLESH
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Undead reforge when combined with Armor.; The Zombie it came from was probably a great warrior before its death.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Health  Defense  Strength  Attack Speed; Undead Bonus Take -2%  Damage from  Undead mobs.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 16.; UNCOMMON REFORGE STONE
Premium Flesh is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Undead]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]], granting [[Health]], [[Defense]], [[Strength]], and [[Attack Speed]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
Premium Flesh has a 0.1% chance to drop from the following mobs in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]:
- [[Crypt Lurker]]
- [[Zombie Grunt]]
## Usage
### Reforging
Premium Flesh can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Undead]] reforge, to grant [[Health]], [[Defense]], [[Strength]], and [[Attack Speed]], which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XVI (16)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Undead]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Undead Bonus, which decreases damage taken from [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] by 2%.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|

|Reforge Bonus|Undead Bonus; Take -2% Damage from Undead mobs.|Undead Bonus; Take -2% Damage from Undead mobs.|Undead Bonus; Take -2% Damage from Undead mobs.|Undead Bonus; Take -2% Damage from Undead mobs.|Undead Bonus; Take -2% Damage from Undead mobs.|Undead Bonus; Take -2% Damage from Undead mobs.|Undead Bonus; Take -2% Damage from Undead mobs.|
|Cost to apply|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VINYL_PRETTY_FLY
Tooltip: Place in a Chirping Stereo to play funky tunes for  Pests.; When playing, this vinyl attracts Fly Pests.; UNCOMMON
The Pretty Fly Vinyl is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Fly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|5%* ([[Pests]])|
## Usage
### Vacuum
The Pretty Fly Vinyl can be used in a [[InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius]] to increase the chance of [[Fly|Flies]] spawning.
### Disco Destination
The Pretty Fly Vinyl can be used in a [[Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination]] to make [[Fly|Flies]] spawn near it while offline.
### Vinyl Set
The Pretty Fly Vinyl can be used in a [[Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set]] to fill it so it can be used to charm [[Garden Visitors]].
## Trivia
- The Pretty Fly Vinyl's name could be a reference to the song Pretty Fly (For A White Guy), recorded by The Offspring for the 1998 album Americana.

The Promising Axe is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] early game [[Axes|Axe]]. It has a special ability that all promising tools have. Every time the player breaks a certain amount of blocks using the axe, it will get one more level of [[Efficiency]], capping at [[Efficiency|Efficiency V]].
## Obtaining
### Quest
Promising Axe can be obtained as a reward for completing [[Lumber Jack]]'s quest.
### Merchant
Promising Axe can be bought from the [[Lumber Merchant]] for 35 [[Coins]].
### Crafting

|None|2x [[Stick]] 3x [[Block of Iron]]|Recipe: [[Block of Iron]] ×3 + [[Stick]] ×2 → Promising Axe|

|50|I|
|250|II|
|750|III|

Promising Pickaxe is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]] that grants [[Mining Speed]] based on the amount of blocks the player has mined with it.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|2x [[Stick]] 3x [[Block of Iron]]|Recipe: [[Block of Iron]] ×3 + [[Stick]] ×2 → Promising Pickaxe|
### Mining Merchant
A Promising Pickaxe can be purchased from the [[Mining Merchant]] for 35 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Pickaxe:
Promising Pickaxe [[Damage]]: +20 [[Breaking Power]]: +3 [[Mining Speed]]: +50
[Effects]
[Grants +10 Mining Speed for every 100 blocks mined.]
For every 100 blocks mined while using the Promising Pickaxe, it gains +10 [[Mining Speed]], up to a maximum of +250 [[Mining Speed]] (2,500 blocks). Blocks mined and the current Mining Speed is tracked in the pickaxe's item lore. While it stops at 250 blocks for Mining Speed, Blocks Mined will continue past the 2,500 blocks required for the maximum amount of [[Mining Speed]].
## Demonstration
Promising Pickaxe Demonstration
## See also
- [[Promising Axe]]
- [[Promising Hoe]]
- [[Promising Shovel]]

# Puff Crux
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Puff Crux]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No

Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell (Motes): 100 [[Motes]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PUFF_CRUX
Tooltip: Pure CO².; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Puff Crux is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item and a crafting material.
## Obtaining
Defeating [[Puff|Puffs]] has a 10% (1 in 10 kills) chance to yield [[Puff Crux]] 1 Puff Crux. This amount can be increased by chaining multiple [[Puff|Puffs]] together before killing them, as well as increasing the player's Crux Fortune through the [[Crux Talisman]] and its upgrades.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Celestial Starstone]]|None|1x [[Crux Chronomicon]] [[Puff Crux]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Puff Crux|Recipe: Puff Crux x16 ×8 + [[Crux Chronomicon]] → [[Celestial Starstone]]|
|[[Respiration Talisman]]|None|[[Puff Crux]] 9x Puff Crux|Recipe: Puff Crux ×9 → [[Respiration Talisman]]|
|[[Respiration Ring]]|None|1x [[Respiration Talisman]] [[Puff Crux]] 28x Puff Crux|Recipe: Puff Crux x4 ×7 + [[Respiration Talisman]] → [[Respiration Ring]]|
|[[Respiration Artifact]]|None|1x [[Respiration Ring]] [[Puff Crux]] 128x Puff Crux|Recipe: Puff Crux x16 ×8 + [[Respiration Ring]] → [[Respiration Artifact]]|
### NPC Trading
[[Puff Crux]] 64 (1 stack) Puff Crux can be used to purchase [[Shen's Ringalia]] from [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X]].
|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|
|-|-|-|
|[[Shen's Ringalia]]|Completion of Eleanor's Puzzle|25,000 [[Motes]] [[Puff Crux]] 64x Puff Crux 20x [[Youngite]] 20x [[Timite]] 20x [[Obsolite]] 1x [[Disinfestor Gloves]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added Puff Crux.|

## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Quake Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] and Combat Category
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher
||
||

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Quake Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Stone Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Stone Elemental ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Strength]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

### Fusion

Tooltip: Legendary Rabbit Luck I Increases your chance of finding LEGENDARY Rabbits during Hoppity's Hunt by +1%.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 5.; UNCOMMON FOREST SHARD (ID U106)
The Rabbit Cat Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Legendary Rabbit Luck]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Rabbit Cat Shard can be purchased from the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]] for 4,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]] after reaching [[Chocolate Factory]] level 3. The player can purchase up to 18 each [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]].
### Fusion
A Rabbit Cat Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Rabbit Cat Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Legendary Rabbit Luck]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Legendary Rabbit Luck ([[Attributes]]); Increases your chance of finding LEGENDARY Rabbits during [[Hoppity's Hunt]] by +1%–10%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Rabbit Cat Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Rabbit Cat Shard has the following description:
- The favorite cat of the Rabbit Godfather. He believes it brings him good luck.

The Radiant Power Orb is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Power Orbs|Power Orb]] unlockable at [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer II (2)]]. It can be upgraded into a [[Mana Flux Power Orb]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wolf Slayer II (2) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|8x [[Enchanted Emerald]] 16x [[Wolf Tooth]]|Recipe: [[Wolf Tooth]] x2 ×8 + [[Enchanted Emerald]] x8 → Radiant Power Orb|
## Usage
Deployable:
Radiant Power Orb
[Effects]
[Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK; Place an orb for 1m buffing up to 5 players within 18 blocks.; Mana Cost: 50% of max; Vitality Cost: 50 Vitality; Orb Buff: Radiant; Heal yourself for 30 Health per second.; Heal others for 15 Health per second.; Gain +25 Health Regen.; Only one deployable buff applies.]
Using its ability does not remove or use up the item. When placed, it cannot be picked up; the ability spawns a new orb.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Mana Flux Power Orb]]|Wolf Slayer LVL VI (6) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|[[Radiant Power Orb]] 1x Radiant Power Orb 4x [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] 32x [[Golden Tooth]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]] ×4 + [[Golden Tooth]] x8 ×4 + Radiant Power Orb → [[Mana Flux Power Orb]]|
## Trivia
- For a while since March 28, 2021, players could place Radiant, [[Mana Flux Power Orb|Manaflux]], [[Overflux Power Orb|Overflux]], and [[Plasmaflux Power Orb|Plasmaflux]] Power Orbs on the private island by shift clicking them on a chest and then breaking the chest, allowing the player to place it as a head. This was patched a month later.

# Raffle Ticket
Aliases: Raffle Tickets
Categories: Uncommon items, Items
[Raffle Ticket]
Type: Event item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Bring this back to the raffle box!
Source: Dwarven Mines Raffle Event
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MINING_RAFFLE_TICKET
Tooltip: Bring this back to the raffle box!; UNCOMMON
Raffle Tickets are tickets that appear on the ground during [[Raffle|Raffle Events]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Raffle Event
During the raffle event, players pick up Raffle Tickets, which spawn on the ground, and turn them into the [[Raffle Box]] for a chance at triple rewards from the event (This event can take place in either the [[Royal Palace]] or the [[Far Reserve]]). They have no use outside this event.
## Trivia
- You cannot use Raffle Tickets from another event. This prevents "cheating" in the event.
- The best way to get Raffle Tickets is to get an [[Armor|Armor Set]] with a lot of [[Speed]].
  - [[Farmer Boots]], [[Rancher's Boots]], [[Young Dragon Armor]], etc.
- Additionally, a [[Black Cat Pet]] is recommended as it provides the highest speed bonus among pets and increases speed cap though any other pet increasing speed is also good for the event.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Raffle Ticket.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Raffle Tickets now despawn when the event is picking a winner.|
